torch\n     6→import torch.distributed as dist\n     7→\n     8→\n     9→@torch.compile(dynamic=True)\n    10→def compute_approx_kl(\n    11→    log_probs: torch.Tensor,\n    12→    log_probs_base: torch.Tensor,\n    13→    kl_loss_type: str,\n    14→) -> torch.Tensor:\n    15→    \"\"\"\n    16→    Compute the approximate KL divergence between two distributions.\n    17→    Schulman blog: http://joschu.net/blog/kl-approx.html\n    18→\n    19→    Args:\n    20→        log_probs: Log probabilities of the new distribution.\n    21→        log_probs_base: Log probabilities of the base distribution.\n    22→        action_mask: Mask for actions.\n    23→    \"\"\"\n    24→\n    25→    log_ratio = log_probs.float() - log_probs_base.float()\n    26→\n    27→    if kl_loss_type == \"kl\":\n    28→        return log_ratio\n    29→    elif kl_loss_type == \"k2\":\n    30→        log_ratio = log_probs.float() - log_probs_base.float()\n    31→        log_ratio = log_ratio**2 / 2.0\n    32→        return log_ratio\n    33→    elif kl_loss_type == \"k3\":\n    34→        log_ratio = -log_ratio\n    35→        log_ratio = log_ratio.exp() - 1 - log_ratio\n    36→        return log_ratio\n    37→    elif kl_loss_type == \"low_var_kl\":\n    38→        # The non negative kl approximation in\n    39→        # http://joschu.net/blog/kl-approx.html\n    40→        # Besides non negative, it is also unbiased and have lower variance.\n    41→        log_ratio = -log_ratio\n    42→        log_ratio = log_ratio.exp() - 1 - log_ratio\n    43→        return torch.clamp(log_ratio, min=-10, max=10)\n    44→    else:\n    45→        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown kl_loss_type: {kl_loss_type}\")\n    46→\n    47→\n    48→@torch.compile(dynamic=True)\n    49→def compute_policy_loss(\n    50→    ppo_kl: torch.Tensor,\n    51→    advantages: torch.Tensor,\n    52→    eps_clip: float,\n    53→    eps_clip_high: float,\n    54→    eps_clip_c: Optional[float] = None,\n    55→):\n    56→    ratio = (-ppo_kl).exp()\n    57→    pg_losses1 = -ratio * advantages\n    58→    pg_losses2 = -ratio.clamp(1 - eps_clip, 1 + eps_clip_high) * advantages\n    59→    clip_pg_losses1 = torch.maximum(pg_losses1, pg_losses2)\n    60→    clipfrac = torch.gt(pg_losses2, pg_losses1).float()\n    61→\n    62→    if eps_clip_c is not None:\n    63→        assert (\n    64→            eps_clip_c > 1.0\n    65→        ), f\"The lower bound of the clip_ratio_c for dual-clip PPO should be greater than 1.0, but get the value: {eps_clip_c}.\"\n    66→        pg_losses3 = -eps_clip_c * advantages\n    67→        clip_pg_losses2 = torch.min(pg_losses3, clip_pg_losses1)\n    68→        pg_losses = torch.where(advantages < 0, clip_pg_losses2, clip_pg_losses1)\n    69→    else:\n    70→        pg_losses = clip_pg_losses1\n    71→\n    72→    return pg_losses, clipfrac\n    73→\n    74→\n    75→def compute_log_probs(logits: torch.Tensor, tokens: torch.Tensor, process_group: Optional[dist.ProcessGroup]):\n    76→    from megatron.core.fusions.fused_cross_entropy import fused_vocab_parallel_cross_entropy\n    77→\n    78→    # convert to [seq_len, batch_size, vocab_size] as expected by fused_vocab_parallel_cross_entropy\n    79→    logits = logits.unsqueeze(1)\n    80→    tokens = tokens.unsqueeze(1)\n    81→    return -fused_vocab_parallel_cross_entropy(logits, _VocabParallelEntropy(torch.autograd.Function):\n    86→\n    87→    @staticmethod\n    88→    def forward(ctx, vocab_parallel_logits: torch.Tensor, process_group: dist.ProcessGroup) -> torch.Tensor:\n    89→\n    90→        @torch.compile(dynamic=True)\n    91→        def mul_reduce(a, b):\n    92→            return (a * b).sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)\n    93→\n    94→        logits_max = vocab_parallel_logits.max(dim=-1, keepdim=True).values\n    95→        dist.all_reduce(logits_max, op=dist.ReduceOp.MAX, group=process_group)\n    96→        normalized_vocab_parallel_logits = vocab_parallel_logits - logits_max\n    97→        normalized_exp_logits = normalized_vocab_parallel_logits.exp_()\n    98→        normalized_sum_exp_logits = normalized_exp_logits.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)\n    99→        dist.all_reduce(normalized_sum_exp_logits, group=process_group)\n   100→        softmax_logits = normalized_exp_logits.div_(normalized_sum_exp_logits)\n   101→        sum_softmax_times_logits = mul_reduce(softmax_logits, vocab_parallel_logits)\n   102→        dist.all_reduce(sum_softmax_times_logits, group=process_group)\n   103→        entropy = logits_max + normalized_sum_exp_logits.log() - sum_softmax_times_logits\n   104→        ctx.save_for_backward(vocab_parallel_logits, softmax_logits, sum_softmax_times_logits)\n   105→        return entropy.squeeze(dim=-1)\n   106→\n   107→    @staticmethod\n   108→    def backward(ctx, grad_output: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\n   109→        vocab_parallel_logits, softmax_logits, sum_softmax_times_logits = ctx.saved_tensors\n   110→        # reuse softmax_logits as grad\n   111→        vocab_parallel_logits.sub_(sum_softmax_times_logits)\n   112→        softmax_logits.mul_(vocab_parallel_logits)\n   113→        softmax_logits.mul_(grad_output.unsqueeze(dim=-1))\n   114→ \"\"\"\n    Compute the approximate KL divergence between two distributions.\n    Schulman blog: http://joschu.net/blog/kl-approx.html\n\n    Args:\n        log_probs: Log probabilities of the new Group rewards by prompt (groups of 8)\n2. Subtract group mean: `r' = r - mean(group)`\n3. Divide by group std: `r'' = r' / (std(group) + 1e-6)`\n\nIf all 8 rewards in a group are identical, then:\n- std = 0\n- r'' = 0 / (0 + 1e-6) = 0\n- All normalized rewards = 0\n- Advantages = 0\n- No gradient signal\n\nThis confirms my analysis. The `rewards_normalization` with `grpo_std_normalization` causes zero gradients when within-batch variance is zero.\n\nNow let me also check: `get_grpo_returns` function:\n```python\ndef get_grpo_returns(rewards, kl):\n    returns = []\n    for i in range(len(rewards)):\n        returns.append(torch.ones_like(kl[i]) * rewards[i])\n    return returns\n```\n\nThis broadcasts the scalar reward to all tokens. When the reward is zero, all returns are zero, and all advantages are zero.\n\nOK, now I'm completely confident.
